Title: Das Leben Jo. Wilhelmi Petersen, der Heil. Schrifft Doctoris, vormahls Professoris zu Rostock, nachgehends Predigers in Hannover, Superintendenten in Lübeck und Lüneburg; von ihm selbst beschrieben, nach seiner grossen Oeconomie in der Wiederbringung aller Dinge
Creator: Johann Wilhelm Petersen (1649-1727)
Publisher: No place given: 'Gedruckt zu Ende des Jahrs 1717, auf Kosten guter Freunde'
Publication Date: 1717
Rights: Public Domain (published 1717, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: deu
Source: Internet Archive item 10071488bsb (https://archive.org/details/10071488bsb), the item's own 10071488bsb_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: not named in the item metadata; host metadata date: 1717. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: e76e5e0532bbf474abce25cd2cbcbd45c9267783d9ec5fb10700391bd5555693.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Bavarian State Library scan; Fraktur OCR with the long s kept, uneven on the title page, readable in the body; two library stamp lines precede the title and are left in place.
Content note: The autobiography of the radical-Pietist theologian Johann Wilhelm Petersen, dismissed from Lüneburg in 1692 for his chiliasm, written by himself; his wife Johanna Eleonora appears in it, but her own Leben (1718) is a separate work not found in the public domain.

Petersen, Johann Wilhelm, 


Das Leben Jo. Wihelmi Petersen, Der Heil. Schrift Doctoris, Vormahls 
Professoris zu Rostock, nachgehends Predigers in Hannover an St. Egi 
Kirche, damach des Bischoffs in Lübeck Superintendentis und Hof-Predigers, 
endlich Superintendentis in Lüneburg, Als Zeugens der Warheit Christ und 

seines Reiches, nach seiner grossen Oeconomie in der Wiederbringung aller
